Conclusion: 通过显式建模维度间干扰并采用顺序奖励调度与自适应提示选择，可有效缓解多维度优化的不平衡与冲突问题，提升 T23D 生成的整体质量与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for Text-to-3D (T23D) generation requires optimization across multiple quality dimensions such as semantic alignment and texture clarity. Existing methods typically optimize these dimensions simultaneously through multiple reward aggregation, without explicitly modeling inter-dimension dependencies. This can cause imbalanced optimization and persistent interference among conflicting dimensions. To address this limitation, we propose OuroReward, an interference-aware sequential reward scheduling strategy for T23D RL. OuroReward first estimates pairwise dependencies among dimensions and constructs a cyclic optimization path that minimizes cumulative interference. By incorporating the tail-to-head dependency, the cycle captures global compatibility across the entire schedule. Then, OuroReward converts the cycle into a one-pass sequence, and starts optimization from the dimension with the lowest aggregate interference. Rather than assigning a fixed optimization budget to each dimension-wise reward, training adaptively determines when to advance to the next reward according to the remaining optimization headroom of the current one. We further introduce AdaSelect, an adaptive prompt selection strategy that identifies reliable and informative prompts aligned with the model's current capability. By focusing policy updates on these prompts, AdaSelect effectively improves training stability. Extensive experiments across different T23D models and RL algorithms demonstrate that our framework consistently improves generation quality across multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [78] [Depth Hypothesis Guided Iterative Refinement for Event-Image Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03439)
*Daikun Liu,Teng Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HypoDepth，首个事件-图像单目深度迭代精化框架，通过离散深度假设体（DHV）将深度回归转化为约束深度搜索，在DSEC和MVSEC上达到SOTA并具实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有优秀动态特性，在单目深度估计（MDE）中潜力巨大。但现有方法主要通过优化上下文特征提升性能，仍难以应对直接全深度回归的病态性与非线性问题。

Method: 引入离散深度假设体（DHV），将深度回归问题转化为受约束的深度搜索任务；构建DHV特征与上下文特征之间的3D代价体，执行多尺度相关性搜索以引导稳定的残差优化；轻量代价体支持多分辨率下的高效全局到局部精化。

Result: 在DSEC和MVSEC数据集上超越现有方法，取得SOTA结果并具备强零样本泛化能力；微型模型在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，可在资源受限设备上实现实时性能。

Conclusion: 通过将深度回归转为约束搜索并引入迭代精化框架，HypoDepth有效缓解了直接全深度回归的病态与非线性难题，兼顾精度、泛化与实时效率。

Abstract: Event cameras hold excellent dynamic properties, showing great potential for monocular depth estimation (MDE). However, existing methods mainly improve performance by optimizing contextual features, but still struggle with the ill-posed and nonlinear nature of direct full-depth regression. In this paper, we propose HypoDepth, the first event-image monocular depth iterative refinement framework. By introducing a discrete Depth Hypothesis Volume (DHV), we transform the depth regression problem into a constrained depth search task. Specifically, we construct a 3D cost volume between the DHV features and contextual features and perform a multi-scale correlation search to guide stable residual optimization. This lightweight cost volume enables efficient global-to-local refinement across multi-resolution. Our method outperforms existing approaches on DSEC and MVSEC with state-of-the-art results and strong zero-shot generalization. Meanwhile, our tiny model achieves an excellent balance between accuracy and efficiency, enabling real-time performance on resource-limited devices.

</details>


### [79] [ChromaGS: Text-Driven Semantic Editing of 4D Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03441)
*Antonio Canela,Jordi Sànchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChromaGS 实现了对可动画 3D 高斯头部化身进行实时、语言引导的颜色编辑，无需重训练，并能在渲染时精确修改语义区域的颜色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式编辑方法容易引入非预期的修改，缺乏精确、局部的语义控制；同时可动画 3D 高斯头部化身的颜色编辑需要保持外观保真度与实时交互性，且避免重训练成本。

Method: 为每个高斯原语引入对语义区域的软分配，并将颜色解耦为区域级基色与高斯级残差：修改区域基色可在相关高斯间一致传播，同时由残差保留细节。另设计两阶段语言流程，将文本指令映射为目标颜色，支持绝对指定与相对调整，编辑在渲染时应用。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多样主体上具有良好的外观保真度、精确的语义定位与直观的交互体验，且支持实时编辑而无需重训练。

Conclusion: ChromaGS 通过区域软分配与颜色分解，实现了确定性强、定位精确的语言引导高斯头部化身颜色编辑，避免了生成式方法的非预期改动。

Abstract: We present ChromaGS, a method for real-time, language-guided color editing of animatable 3D Gaussian head avatars. Given a trained animatable avatar, users can instantly modify the color of semantic regions through natural language, with edits applied at render time and no retraining required. Our key insight is to augment each Gaussian primitive with learned soft assignments to semantic regions and decompose colors into region-level base colors and Gaussian-level residuals. This decomposition enables coherent color transfer: modifying a region's base color propagates naturally through all associated Gaussians while preserving fine appearance details encoded in residuals. A two-stage language pipeline translates text instructions into target colors, supporting both absolute specifications and relative adjustments. Unlike generative editing methods that may introduce unintended modifications, our approach provides deterministic, precisely localized semantic control. Experiments demonstrate faithful appearance preservation and intuitive interaction across diverse subjects. Project page and code are available at: https://a-canela.github.io/chromags/

</details>


### [80] [Corrupted but Correct: Why Vision-Language Models Lie to Themselves Internally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03445)
*Arun Josephraj Arokiaraj,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉语言模型(VLM)中"训练/推理鸿沟"现象:定向对抗扰动可使教师强制下目标字幕损失降为零,但自由生成时模型仍输出正确描述。作者在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct上通过两阶段PGD攻击200张COCO图像,发现图像级像素统计几乎无预测力,鸿沟定位于单个自回归步骤,且源于语言解码器的差异性仲裁而非视觉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在大模型时代被广泛部署,但其对抗鲁棒性与忠实性评测的关键瓶颈尚不明确。作者注意到一个反直觉现象:定向对抗扰动能让教师强制训练损失趋近于零,而同一模型自由生成时却毫无目标痕迹,这种训练与推理的脱节(train/inference gap)机制不清,亟需精确解释以指导防御与评测。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct上用受控两阶段PGD攻击200张留出的COCO图像;检验图像级像素统计(含重新实现的基于纹理的可攻击性度量)的预测力;利用logit lens定位鸿沟到具体自回归步骤;追踪全部28层LLM解码器中目标token排名;用线性探针在merger隐状态上区分两种结果。

Result: 图像级像素统计几乎无预测力(最佳预测器r=-0.050,p=0.484;岭回归R^2=0.069)。鸿沟定位于单个自回归步骤:在已生成正确首token条件下,目标token排名恒为3,488/152,064,零方差。视觉编码器对每张图像的表示破坏幅度相近,而语言解码器差异化仲裁:对易感图像放大被污染信号,对抵抗图像主动抑制至低于干净基线(p<0.001,rank-biserial r=0.579)。merger隐状态线性探针分离两种结果AUC=0.858(作者标注存在循环性隐忧)。

Conclusion: 自回归VLM的对抗鲁棒性在很大程度上是语言解码器先验的属性,而非视觉编码器的属性。这为VLM系统的忠实性评测与防御应聚焦何处提供了直接启示,即应针对语言解码器而非视觉编码器。

Abstract: A targeted adversarial perturbation can drive a vision-language model's (VLM's) teacher-forced training loss for a fixed target caption to near zero, yet the same model, allowed to generate freely, produces the original, correct description with no trace of the target. We call this dissociation the train/inference gap, and give it a precise mechanistic account on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct using a controlled two-stage PGD attack on 200 held-out COCO images. First, we show that image-level pixel statistics, including a correctly re-implemented, texture-based attackability measure from the CNN robustness literature, have essentially no predictive power over which images are corrupted (best predictor r=-0.050, p=0.484; ridge regression R^2=0.069). Second, using the logit lens, we localise the gap to a single autoregressive step: the rank of the target token, conditioned on the correct first token already being generated, is fixed at exactly 3,488 out of 152,064 vocabulary entries for every image and every condition, with zero variance. Third, tracking target-token rank across all 28 LLM decoder layers reveals that the visual encoder corrupts every image's representation by a comparable margin regardless of eventual outcome, but the language model decoder then differentially arbitrates: amplifying the corrupted signal for susceptible images and actively suppressing it, past its clean-image baseline, for resistant ones (p<0.001, rank-biserial r=0.579). A linear probe on the merger hidden state separates these two outcomes with AUC=0.858, though we flag a circularity concern in this estimate. Together these results argue that adversarial robustness in autoregressive VLMs is substantially a property of the language decoder's prior, not the visual encoder, with direct implications for where faithfulness evaluations and defenses for deployed VLM systems should be targeted.

</details>


### [81] [Preserving Anatomical Continuity: Three-Stage Pipeline for Colon Segmentation in 3D Abdominal CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03467)
*Deshan Kalupahana,Sonit Singh,Praveen Ravindran,Arcot Sowmya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段拓扑保持分割流程，通过中心线桥接与重建提升结肠分割的连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT结肠分割对结直肠疾病分析至关重要，但复杂解剖结构导致深度学习分割易产生不连通的预测结果。

Method: 三阶段流程：第一阶段基于深度学习进行初始分割；随后通过中心线桥接重新连接不连通区域；最后通过重建阶段细化连续性。

Result: 在TotalSegmentator和RAOS数据集上，使用重叠、距离及拓扑类指标评估，在保持分割精度的同时提升了结构一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了拓扑完整性，可为临床与研究应用提供更可靠的结肠分割结果。

Abstract: Accurate colon segmentation from CT images is essential for colorectal disease analysis, yet deep learning based methods often produce disconnected predictions due to complex anatomy. This study introduces a three-stage, topology-preserving segmentation pipeline to address this issue. The first stage performs initial deep learning-based segmentation, followed by centreline bridging to reconnect disjoint regions and a reconstruction stage to refine continuity. Evaluations on TotalSegmentator and RAOS datasets using overlap, distance and topology-based metrics demonstrate improved structural consistency while maintaining segmentation accuracy. The proposed method enhances topological integrity, enabling more reliable colon segmentation for clinical and research applications.

</details>


### [82] [A Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based Pipeline for End-to-End Procedural Modeling of Field-Grown Maize from Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03468)
*Mozhgan Hadadi,Talukder Z. Jubery,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化流水线，可从玉米田块三维点云重建程序化可编辑3D植株模型，无需人工调参或物种特异性训练数据，中位全株Chamfer距离为5.4 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编辑3D作物模型支持高通量表型分析与计算机育种试验，但从扫描点云构建需器官级分割与拟合；过程化生成器虽可将器官参数转为可用模型，却需每株数小时人工调参或物种特异性分割标签，成本高、难扩展。

Method: 多模态视觉语言模型（VLM）在正交渲染视图中标注叶片中线；确定性几何算法将标注反投影到点云，通过跨视图一致性合并为3D叶片，并在方向加权表面图上将中线生长为完整叶面。测量得到的器官参数填入植株描述符，驱动基于NURBS的程序化模型生成器；再用可微NURBS拟合将各叶面精配到扫描点。

Result: 在MaizeField3D的100株基因型多样田块玉米上，全株Chamfer距离中位数5.4 mm，优于基于人工标注的半自动流水线；在未使用标签作为输入的情况下，以IoU≥0.5恢复了1,023片参考叶中的1,017片（99.4%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当下游几何阶段能纠正VLM标注时，其可作为可用的器官级测量，使现代表型实验规模下的可编辑3D植株资产自动生成成为可能。

Abstract: Editable 3D models of field-grown crops support high-throughput phenotyping and in silico breeding trials, but building them from scanned point clouds requires organ-level segmentation and fitting. Procedural generators can turn an organ-level parameter set into an analysis-suitable 3D model, but obtaining that set requires hours of manual tuning per plant or segmentation models trained on species-specific labels. We present an automated pipeline that reconstructs procedural maize models from raw 3D point clouds without manual tuning or species-specific training data. A multimodal vision-language model (VLM) annotates leaf midlines in rendered orthographic views. Deterministic geometric algorithms back-project the annotations onto the point cloud, merge them into 3D leaves by cross-view consensus, and grow the midlines to full blades on an orientation-weighted surface graph. Measured organ parameters populate a plant descriptor for a Non-Uniform Rational B-Spline (NURBS)-based procedural model generator. Each leaf surface is then refined against its scan points by differentiable NURBS fitting. The pipeline reached a median whole-plant Chamfer distance of 5.4 mm on 100 genotypically diverse field-grown maize plants from the MaizeField3D dataset. The reconstructions were closer to the scans than those of an earlier semi-automated pipeline based on manual annotations. The pipeline recovered 1,017 of 1,023 (99.4%) curated reference leaves at an intersection-over-union of at least 0.5 without using those labels as input. These results show that VLM annotations become usable organ-level measurements when downstream geometric stages can correct them. This makes automated generation of editable 3D plant assets feasible at the scale of modern phenotyping experiments.

</details>


### [83] [UniDynamics: Event-RGB Fusion for Unified Future 4D Dynamic Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03473)
*Daikun Liu,Xin Zhan,Teng Wang,Xiaoping Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniDynamics 扩散框架，从单对事件-RGB 输入生成未来 4D 动态场景（RGB、深度、光流），无需长历史或控制先验，通过事件流提供运动先验并全程施加几何与运动约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未来 4D 动态场景生成方法依赖长历史序列或控制先验，限制了单帧外推场景下的应用；同时未来运动场建模不足，难以保证几何与运动一致性。

Method: 设计事件潜在增强（ELE）模块，对齐并增强事件潜在特征为可注入扩散的条件特征，提供初始运动先验及纹理/结构线索；引入嵌入多尺度 U-Net 的感知动力学空间（PDS），解耦并自适应交互深度与光流，并持续向外观特征反馈约束。

Result: 在 VKitti2 和 DSEC 数据集上达到最先进性能，生成高质量、时间连贯、4D 一致的未来预测，尤其在高速度运动模糊等挑战场景下表现优越。

Conclusion: 通过事件流作为运动先验并结合多模态建模约束，UniDynamics 实现了无需长历史或控制先验的单对事件-RGB 4D 动态场景生成，显著提升几何-运动一致性。

Abstract: We propose UniDynamics, a diffusion-based framework for future 4D dynamic scenes (RGB, depth, and optical flow) generation from a single event-RGB pair, without requiring long histories or control priors as in existing methods, while explicitly modeling future motion fields. The core idea is to leverage event streams to offer an alternative motion prior for single-RGB extrapolation, and to enforce geometric and motion constraints throughout generation via multimodal modeling. Specifically, we design an Event Latent Enhancement (ELE) module to align and enhance event latents into diffusion-injectable conditioning features, providing robust initial motion priors and reliable texture/structure cues. We further introduce a Perceptual Dynamics Space (PDS) embedded in the multi-scale U-Net, which decouples and adaptively interacts depth and flow while continuously feeding back constraints to appearance features, improving geometric-motion consistency for physically plausible and spatiotemporally coherent prediction. Experiments on VKitti2 and DSEC demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, producing high-quality, temporally coherent, and 4D-consistent future predictions, especially under challenging high-speed motion blur.

</details>


### [84] [Fed-ADApt: Federated Anytime Depth Adaptation for Resource-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03474)
*Abhijeet Parida,Zhifan Jiang,Pooneh Roshanitabrizi,Austin Tapp,Maria J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Syed Muhammad Anwar,Ziyue Xu,Marius George Linguraru,Holger R. Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fed-ADApt，一种深度自适应联邦学习框架，通过多深度监督和分层深度聚合，让不同算力机构共同训练UNet分割模型并支持部署时动态深度选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法假设各机构算力同质，限制了低资源站点的参与，同时未兼顾训练与推理的算力约束。

Method: Fed-ADApt结合多深度监督与分层深度聚合，使各站点按本地算力预算训练，并汇聚为支持部署时动态深度选择的全局模型。

Result: 在2D视网膜眼底视盘分割与3D脑肿瘤分割上，联邦协作显著提升域偏移鲁棒性。3D任务匹配全资源FedAvg性能，2D平均Dice仅降4.7%，推理成本3D降19.5%、2D降34.5%，最受限站点训练成本降98%。

Conclusion: Fed-ADApt让低资源机构也能参与联邦训练并保持有竞争力的全局性能，在精度与效率间取得良好折衷，为异构临床与边缘影像环境提供实用且公平的联邦分割方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training of medical image segmentation models without sharing raw patient data, yet existing approaches assume a homogeneous compute budget across institutions, limiting participation of low-resource sites. We propose Fed-ADApt, a depth-adaptive federated framework for UNet-based segmentation that jointly addresses low-compute training and inference. Fed-ADApt integrates multi-depth supervision with hierarchical depth-wise aggregation, allowing each site to train according to its local compute budget while contributing to a global model that supports dynamic depth selection at deployment. We evaluated Fed-ADApt on multi-site 2D retinal fundus disc segmentation and 3D brain tumor segmentation. Across both tasks, federated collaboration substantially improves robustness under domain shift. Fed-ADApt matched the full-resource FedAvg performance in 3D and achieved competitive 2D performance with a 4.7% average Dice reduction, while reducing average inference cost by 19.5% in 3D and 34.5% in 2D and substantially reducing training cost by 98% at the most constrained sites. Importantly, Fed-ADApt enables low-resource institutions that cannot train full-capacity models to participate in federations while maintaining competitive global performance under a favorable accuracy to efficiency trade-off. By considering training and inference compute budgets, Fed-ADApt provides a practical and equitable solution for federated medical image segmentation across heterogeneous clinical and edge-enabled imaging environments.

</details>


### [85] [Weave Forcing: Compositional Memory Routing for Interactive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03510)
*Ziyi Wang,Junchi Yao,Heqian Qiu,Wenbo Shi,Chengjiu Wang,Jinyang He,Binkai Hong,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Weave Forcing，一个免训练框架，通过语义槽路由与掩码记忆编织，实现交互式长视频生成中的组合式记忆复用，提升跨镜头主体与背景一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式叙事需要新镜头能组合来自不同历史镜头的角色与背景，而现有自回归视频生成仅关注连续场景延展：整体提示检索忽略各组件独立的参考需求，直接拼接全部历史记忆又会引入无关视觉内容。

Method: 1) 用 LLM 进行语义槽路由，将用户提示分解为角色与背景描述，并分别为各组件挑选合适的历史参考；2) 掩码记忆编织：基于语义槽条件构造对比注意力图以生成精细语义掩码，从压缩的历史 KV 记忆中选择性暴露相关 token 指导当前镜头生成；3) 覆盖自适应 RoPE：依据无、部分或全部参考覆盖情况调整时间偏移与记忆保留，缓解不完整历史参考靠近当前生成时的视觉伪影。

Result: 大量实验表明，Weave Forcing 在保持有竞争力的视觉质量与文本对齐的同时，改善了跨镜头的主体与背景一致性。

Conclusion: Weave Forcing 以免训练方式实现组合式记忆复用，为交互式长视频生成提供了一种有效解决组件级参考与记忆干扰问题的方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in autoregressive video generation have improved temporal consistency over extended durations, yet interactive storytelling requires more than continuous scene extension: a new shot may combine characters and backgrounds from different historical shots. Whole prompt retrieval can overlook the distinct reference needs of individual components, while directly combining all historical memories may introduce unrelated visual content. To address these problems, we present Weave Forcing, a training-free framework for compositional memory reuse in interactive long video generation. First, we use an LLM for semantic slot routing to decompose user prompts into character and background descriptions and explicitly select suitable historical references for each component. To isolate the required content, masked memory weaving uses contrasting attention maps conditioned on semantic slots to construct refined semantic masks, selectively exposing relevant tokens from compressed historical KV memories to guide the generation of the current shot. We further introduce coverage adaptive RoPE to adjust temporal offsets and memory retention according to no, partial, or full reference coverage, addressing visual artifacts observed when incomplete historical references are positioned close to the current generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Weave Forcing improves cross-shot subject and background consistency while maintaining competitive visual quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [86] [ProgressNet: Sketching and Prompting with a Frozen Text-to-Image Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03512)
*Arkaprabha Basu,Chaitat Utintu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProgressNet 是一个免训练框架，使冻结的文生图模型能跟随绘制过程逐步生成图像，每轮约一秒，无需新参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类绘画是渐进式的：几笔、查看效果、擦除、修改提示。而现有图像生成器通常接收完成稿一次性生成，任何编辑都从头开始；能跨轮保持状态的方法由文本驱动、无法接收笔画，且速度太慢。作者希望让冻结的文生图模型能实时跟随绘制会话。

Method: 提出 ProgressNet，一种免训练框架，利用冻结模型本身已有的能力（记住上一轮的路径、在不冻结结构的情况下传递外观的层、以及判断未完成草图可信度的内部信号），通过三种推理期机制实现：Previous-Concept Memory（上一概念记忆）、Layer-Selective K/V Injection（层选择性 K/V 注入）和 Banded Adaptive Control（分段自适应控制）。

Result: 在 FS-COCO 上，随草图完成度提高所有现有方法性能下降，FLUX+ControlNet 基线的 FID 在 10% 到 100% 完成度间翻倍，而 ProgressNet 几乎不变；在三个草图领域保持强保真度和渐进一致性，用户偏好度高于五个竞品，在擦除情形下优势最明显。

Conclusion: ProgressNet 无需训练即可让冻结文生图模型支持渐进式、可编辑、近实时的绘制过程，在保真度、渐进一致性和用户偏好上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Humans draw progressively: a few strokes, a look at the result, a stroke erased, a prompt revised. Image generators do not work this way. They typically take a finished sketch and produce the image in a single pass, so every edit starts the picture again, and the models that do keep state across turns are driven by text, cannot take a stroke, and are too slow to draw with. We present ProgressNet, a training-free framework that lets a frozen text-to-image model follow a drawing session as it unfolds: strokes are added and erased, the prompt is revised, and the image keeps up at about a second per turn. It needs no new parameters because the frozen model already has what a progressive generator needs, a pathway through which the previous turn can be remembered, layers that can carry appearance forward without freezing structure, and an internal signal of how far to trust an unfinished sketch; three inference-time mechanisms (Previous-Concept Memory, Layer-Selective K/V Injection and Banded Adaptive Control) use each in turn. As a sketch fills in, every existing method degrades, the FID of the FLUX+ControlNet baseline doubling between 10% and 100% completion on FS-COCO, while ProgressNet's barely moves; it maintains strong fidelity and progressive coherence across three sketch domains and is preferred by users over five competitors, most widely on erasure.

</details>


### [87] [Feedforward Novel View Synthesis for Heterogeneous Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03522)
*Meng Wei,Cheng Zhang,Boying Li,Yihang Chen,Jianmin Zheng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对异构中心相机的前馈新视角合成，本文通过引入token中心相对相机位置编码、局部raymap和投影感知2D RoPE，缓解了token聚合像素光线带来的歧义，并在多相机评测中取得优于基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈新视角合成方法多假设上下文与目标视角共享固定相机类型，这一同构相机假设在实际多传感器系统（透视、鱼眼、全景相机共存，且目标投影可能训练时未见）中失效。同时，token化会引入关键歧义：视觉token聚合的是依赖投影的像素光线束，而现有相机编码主要只提供绝对光线或token中心关系。

Method: 结合token中心相对相机位置编码与所提出的局部raymap（token级表示，显式描述每个token所概括的patch内光线分布）；进一步提出投影感知2D RoPE，用光线诱导的角坐标替代原始图像网格坐标，使相对位置推理在不同相机投影间对齐。这些组件将多样相机视为共享光线空间中的已标定采样。

Result: 在ScanNet++异构相机系统上，该方法在混合相机评测中优于相机条件基线，并展现出对全景视角的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将异构相机统一建模为共享光线空间的标定采样，并显式编码token内光线分布与投影对齐的相对位置，可有效实现跨异构中心相机的前馈新视角合成，并具备向未见投影（如全景）泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Feed-forward novel view synthesis has recently shown promising results from sparse posed images, but most existing methods assume that context and target views share a fixed camera family. This homogeneous-camera assumption breaks in practical multi-sensor systems, where perspective, fisheye, and panoramic cameras may coexist and where the target projection may be unseen during training. We study feed-forward NVS across heterogeneous central cameras and identify a key ambiguity introduced by tokenization: a visual token aggregates a projection-dependent bundle of pixel rays, while existing camera encodings mainly expose absolute rays or token-center relations. To address this, we combine token-center relative Camera Positional Encodings and proposed local raymaps, a token-level representation that explicitly describes the intra-patch ray distribution summarized by each token. We further propose projection-aware 2D RoPE, which replaces raw image-grid coordinates with ray-induced angular coordinates so that relative positional reasoning is aligned across camera projections. Together, these components treat diverse cameras as calibrated samplings of a shared ray space rather than separate visual domains. On ScanNet++ with heterogeneous-camera system, our method improves over camera-conditioned baselines under mixed-camera evaluation and demonstrates zero-shot generalization to panoramic views.

</details>


### [88] [DuoMatching: Joint-Marginal Distribution Matching for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03543)
*Jiahao Zhan,Yan Wang,Yongrui Ma,Qunliang Xing,Ruchang Yao,Runtao Liu,Shijie Zhao,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DuoMatching分布匹配框架，通过联合-边缘统一建模提升流式视频生成的视觉质量与语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）的流式视频生成虽通过联合匹配缓解自回归漂移，但在视觉质量与语义对齐上仍存在不足。

Method: 在联合匹配目标基础上增加边缘匹配目标，借助图像生成器提供帧级监督以迁移互补的视觉与语义先验；引入LatentBridge解决视频学生与图像教师间的潜表示不匹配；并提出Latent Variation Sampling将帧级监督分散到不同时间段以降低冗余。

Result: 实验表明DuoMatching在提升视觉质量、构图与语义对齐的同时基本保持运动动态；人类评估中相对所有对比基线偏好率均超过80%。

Conclusion: 联合-边缘统一的分布匹配框架可有效弥补联合匹配的不足，为流式视频生成提供更强的质量与语义监督。

Abstract: Streaming video generation has benefited from distribution matching distillation (DMD), which matches the joint distribution of video frames to a video teacher's approximation of the real video distribution. Although this joint matching mitigates drift during autoregressive rollouts, limitations remain in visual quality and semantic alignment. To address these limitations, we propose DuoMatching, a distribution matching framework that approximates the real video distribution through a unified joint-marginal formulation. On top of existing joint matching formulations, the additional marginal matching objective provides dedicated frame-level supervision from an image generator, transferring complementary visual and semantic priors from it. To apply this frame-level supervision in video generation, we introduce LatentBridge to resolve the latent representation mismatch between the video student and the image teacher. Latent Variation Sampling further distributes such frame-level supervision across distinct temporal segments, reducing redundancy. Experiments demonstrate that DuoMatching improves visual quality, composition, and semantic alignment while largely preserving motion dynamics. Human evaluations show overall preference rates above 80% against all evaluated baselines. The project page is available at https://johnzhan2023.github.io/DuoMatching/.

</details>


### [89] [Rethinking What to Cache in Few-Step Diffusion Transformers: Solver-Aware Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03577)
*Shuo Yang,Lihao Fang,Yi Zhang,Haixiang Wang,Xincheng Ye,Shufan Chen,Jipeng Guo,Youqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoTarget，一种为给定模型、求解器和复用调度自动选择最佳缓存张量的方法，用于加速蒸馏后的DiT采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT生成样本需要多次昂贵的前向计算。步蒸馏减少了采样步数，但使相邻步间隔变大；缓存方法通过复用早期步骤张量跳过部分评估，然而间隔变大后复用误差增加，因此"缓存什么张量"变得尤为关键。现有方法大多预先固定复用目标，缺乏针对性的选择依据。

Method: AutoTarget使用一小组不含缓存复用的运行来测量复用每个候选张量所带来的误差，然后选择误差最低的候选。此外，作者分析了单个复用步骤的误差如何影响最终样本：针对Euler采样，识别出能产生相同轨迹的缓存目标，并说明存储的求解器更新为何可能不行。

Result: 在蒸馏图像和视频DiT上的实验表明，最佳缓存目标随模型、图像分辨率和求解器而变化。AutoTarget减少了DiT评估次数并降低了缓存存储需求，生成质量接近对应的无缓存运行。在PixArt-LCM和FLUX.1-schnell设置上，其校准排名与留出的缓存运行排名一致。

Conclusion: 缓存目标的选择应随模型、分辨率与求解器自适应确定；AutoTarget通过误差测量实现这一选择，兼顾加速与质量。核心实现已开源以便复现。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can generate high-quality images and videos, but generating each sample requires multiple costly DiT forward passes. Two common ways to accelerate DiT sampling are step distillation, which reduces the number of sampling steps, and caching, which skips some DiT evaluations by reusing a tensor computed at an earlier step. Most caching methods decide in advance which tensor to reuse. After distillation, adjacent sampling steps are farther apart. Reusing a tensor across this larger gap introduces more error, so choosing what to cache becomes especially important. We therefore introduce AutoTarget, a method that chooses the cached tensor for a given model, solver, and reuse schedule. AutoTarget uses a small set of runs without cache reuse to measure the error caused by reusing each candidate tensor, then selects the candidate with the lowest error. We also analyze how an error at one reuse step affects the final sample. For Euler sampling, we identify cache targets that produce the same trajectory and show why a stored solver update may not. Experiments on distilled image and video DiTs show that the best cache target changes with the model, image resolution, and solver. AutoTarget reduces DiT evaluations and retained cache storage. Generation quality remains close to the corresponding uncached run. On the tested PixArt-LCM and FLUX.1-schnell settings, its calibration ranking matches the ranking from held-out cached runs. To help others reproduce the method, we provide its core implementation on GitHub at https://github.com/wali1024-offical/AutoTarget.

</details>


### [90] [ManifoldSplat: Language-Guided Semantic Shape Editing of 3D Gaussian Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03599)
*Antonio Canela,Jordi Sànchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ManifoldSplat 是首个端到端、语言引导的可动画3D高斯化身语义形状编辑框架，在FLAME流形上进行编辑并引入DeltaRegion，实现约90秒重建编辑与约800 FPS渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的3D头部化身编辑难以实现细粒度局部控制，常导致特征纠缠或几何不一致；通过自然语言修改几何需逐提示慢速优化，或牺牲身份与绑定一致性。

Method: 不直接优化非结构化高斯点云，而是在结构化的FLAME流形内执行编辑以严格保持身份与动画；提出DeltaRegion——按区域解耦的条件变分自编码器（CVAE），提供前馈式形状增量，并配合细化阶段恢复视角一致的细节。

Result: 在消费级GPU上约90秒完成化身的重建与编辑，渲染约800 FPS；大量评估显示在局部提示对齐、几何连贯性与身份保持方面达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过将语言引导的语义形状编辑置于结构化FLAME流形中并结合区域解耦的前馈增量生成，ManifoldSplat 高效实现了高质量、可动画3D高斯头化的细粒度可控编辑。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D head avatars have reached near-photorealistic quality. While recent methods enable text-driven manipulation, they struggle to provide fine-grained localized control, often entangling features or lacking geometric consistency. Modifying geometry through natural language currently requires slow per-prompt optimization or compromises identity and rigging. We present ManifoldSplat, the first end-toend framework for language-guided semantic shape editing of animatable 3D Gaussian Splatting avatars reconstructed from monocular videos. By performing edits within the structured FLAME manifold rather than directly optimizing an unstructured Gaussian cloud, we strictly preserve identity and animation. We introduce DeltaRegion, a per-region disentangled Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) delivering feedforward shape deltas, alongside a refining stage to recover view-consistent details. ManifoldSplat reconstructs and edits an avatar in ~90 seconds on a consumer GPU, rendering at ~800 FPS. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our approach sets a new state-of-the-art in localized prompt alignment, geometric coherence, and identity preservation. Project page and code: https://a-canela.github.io/manifoldsplat/

</details>


### [91] [DEPICT: Scoring Text-to-Image Alignment by Answer Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03617)
*Vasco Ramos,Sandra Godinho Silva,Joao Magalhaes,Ricardo Rei,Pedro Henrique Martins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的图像-文本对齐度量DEPICT，用期望一致性替代固定参考答案，结合分解与整体分数，在多基准多骨干上超越所有免训练指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-文本对齐是核心问题，用于字幕评价、幻觉检测、数据筛选和T2I生成器基准测试。随T2I模型进步，评测需能发现缺失物体、属性错换、计数错误、忽略否定等问题。现有方法不足：微调度量绑定于单一骨干和训练分布；整体度量漏掉细粒度细节；分解度量依赖"固定为是"假设，该假设失败时会惩罚忠实图像。

Method: DEPICT为免训练度量：用图像作答与仅凭字幕作答之间的期望一致性替代固定参考答案，并按字幕对问题的决定程度对问题加权；再将该一致性分数与整体分数融合，以恢复分解过程中丢失的上下文。

Result: 用一致性规则替代固定参考答案后，否定准确率从19%提升到88%。在五个基准、三个模型家族共十一个骨干上评测，DEPICT超越所有免训练度量，并在三个人工相关性基准中的两个上超过微调评测器。

Conclusion: DEPICT以期望一致性替代固定参考，兼顾细粒度与整体信息，且免训练、跨骨干通用，是更强且更可迁移的图像-文本对齐度量。

Abstract: Image-text alignment is a core problem in computer vision with applications in caption evaluation, hallucination detection, data curation, and the benchmarking of text-to-image (T2I) generators. As T2I models improve, benchmarking has become demanding, requiring metrics capable of finding a series of issues like missing objects, swapped attributes, miscounts, and ignored negations. Recent work addresses this by fine-tuning evaluators on preference data or by prompting a vision-language model, either holistically with the caption or with decomposed verification questions. However, existing approaches fall short: fine-tuned metrics remain bound to one backbone and training distribution; holistic metrics miss fine-grained details; and decomposed metrics rely on a fixed-YES assumption that penalizes faithful images whenever that assumption fails. In contrast, we propose DEPICT, a training-free metric that replaces fixed reference answers with expected agreement between image-based and caption-only answers, weighting questions by how decisively the caption determines them. By replacing fixed references, our agreement rule increases negation accuracy from 19% to 88%. To recover the context lost during decomposition, DEPICT merges this agreement score with a holistic score. We evaluate DEPICT on five benchmarks and eleven backbones from three model families and find that it surpasses all training-free metrics and exceeds fine-tuned evaluators on two out of three human-correlation benchmarks.

</details>


### [92] [World Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03632)
*Yiqi Liu,Ruifeng Yuan,Yang Wang,Long Li,Fengyu Cai,Hou Pong Chan,Jialin Yu,Hao Zhang,Chenghua Lin,Chenghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 World Embedding Benchmark，用 8,000 个可控物理仿真案例评测视频表征中的物理信息编码，发现跨模态对齐与定量物理信息可恢复性之间存在权衡，并证明物理表征可用于提升视频生成的物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型与视频生成日益重视物理保真度，但视频表征究竟如何编码物理信息仍缺乏系统理解，且现有评测未区分"跨模态物理对齐"与"定量物理信息可恢复性"这两类能力。

Method: 构建 World Embedding Benchmark，包含来自 80 个物理家族的 8,000 个受控仿真案例，覆盖流体力学、固体力学、动力学、光学与电磁学；每个案例提供渲染视频与仿真物理标注，支持三项任务：文本-视频检索、物理属性回归、多选视频-描述对分类。在此基准上评测预训练全模态嵌入模型、轻量探针，并进行物理专用视频-文本对的持续对比训练。

Result: 预训练全模态嵌入模型检索能力弱、族内配对分类接近随机；冻结视频嵌入经轻量探针可恢复有用物理信息；物理对比训练提升了检索与配对分类，但损害物理属性回归；用嵌入为 MiniMax-H3 检索增强生成提供参考视频，可提升生成视频物理保真度，且检索模型越强提升越大。

Conclusion: 物理对齐与物理属性可恢复性需联合评测；二者存在权衡；物理表征对提升视频生成具有实用价值。

Abstract: Physical fidelity has received increasing attention in world models and video generation, yet how video representations encode physical information remains less understood. We introduce the World Embedding Benchmark, comprising 8,000 controlled simulation cases from 80 families spanning fluid mechanics, solid mechanics, dynamics, and optics & electromagnetism. Each case pairs a rendered video with simulation-derived physical annotations, supporting three complementary tasks: text-video retrieval, physical-property regression, and multiple-choice video-description pair classification. We use these tasks to distinguish cross-modal physical alignment from the recoverability of quantitative physical information. Evaluated pre-trained omnimodal embedding models show weak retrieval and near-chance within-family pair classification, while lightweight probes recover useful physical information from frozen video embeddings. Continual contrastive training with physics-specific video-text pairs improves retrieval and pair classification but degrades physical-property regression, revealing a trade-off between alignment and quantitative information recoverability. Finally, we use the embeddings to retrieve reference videos for retrieval-augmented generation with MiniMax-H3. Retrieved references improve the physical fidelity of generated videos, with stronger retrieval models yielding larger gains in our experiments. Together, these findings highlight the need to evaluate physical alignment and property recoverability jointly, and demonstrate the utility of physical representations for improving video generation.

</details>


### [93] [LoGo: Local-Global Rewards for Consistent Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03636)
*Ziqi Ma,Shreya Sharma,Mohamed El Banani,Katja Schwarz,Chongjie Ye,Chao-Yuan Wu,Li Fei-Fei,Ben Mildenhall,Georgia Gkioxari,Justin Johnson,Gowthami Somepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoGo 通过融合全局与局部空间奖励，为相机控制视频模型提供细粒度信用分配，显著提升长时序生成的3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机控制视频模型在长时序与复杂相机运动下易出现3D不一致，物体失去恒常性、场景结构漂移。现有后训练方法对整段生成只给一个标量奖励，难以在长时序上纠正此类不一致。

Method: 提出 LoGo，混合全局奖励与空间局部化奖励。局部奖励提供细粒度信用分配以提升3D一致性，全局奖励维持相机跟随能力与视频质量。并在三个基础模型上验证。

Result: 在 DL3DV 与新建的 TrajectoryBench（面向长时序、复杂相机控制生成的基准）上，LoGo 相较基线具有明显优势，有效减少局部物体偏移、伪影与全局场景变化。

Conclusion: 信用分配机制对视频模型后训练至关重要，全局与局部奖励的融合是提升相机控制视频3D一致性的有效途径。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video models are rapidly advancing toward long generation horizons and complex camera control. A key failure mode is 3D inconsistency: as the camera moves, objects lose permanence and scene structures shift. Existing post-training techniques, which assign a single scalar reward to the entire generation, are poorly suited to correcting these inconsistencies over long horizons. We introduce LoGo, which blends global and spatially localized rewards for camera-controlled video models. The local reward provides fine-grained credit assignment, which substantially improves 3D consistency, while the global reward preserves camera following and video quality. Across three base models, LoGo shows a clear advantage on DL3DV and TrajectoryBench, a new benchmark for long-horizon, complex-camera-control generation that current evaluations lack. LoGo effectively reduces local object shifts, artifacts, and global scene changes, illustrating the importance of credit assignment in post-training video models. Project website: https://ziqi-ma.github.io/logo-website/

</details>


### [94] [On-Board Anomaly Detection for Efficient Marine Environmental Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03649)
*Thomas Goudemant,Clotilde Szywala,Benjamin Francesconi,Michelle Aubrun,Yves Bobichon,Marjorie Bellizzi,Adrien Girard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多/高光谱卫星的海洋事件检测流水线，含自监督编码器与异常检测模型，实现星上高效处理与带宽优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋生态系统受溢油、藻华、泥沙泛滥等威胁，需借助卫星遥感与AI提升早期检测和预警能力，但星上计算资源有限。

Method: 构建自监督神经网络编码器将卫星图像压缩到低维隐空间，配合机器学习异常检测模型识别与正常海况的偏差，并与Isolation Forest、One-Class SVM、Local Outlier Factor等传统算法对比；流水线轻量、资源高效，可部署于嵌入式CPU到AI加速器。

Result: 轻量流水线适配有限算力平台，通过优先传输关键信息提升系统响应速度并优化卫星通信带宽，已在ESA Phisat-2及Microsoft/Thales Alenia Space IMAGIN-e等任务中集成验证。

Conclusion: 该方案可提升海洋环境监测能力，提供及时预警与高效数据压缩，适用于多任务星上部署。

Abstract: Marine ecosystems are impacted by various threats such as oil spills, algal blooms, and sediment floods, which disrupt habitats, wildlife, and human activities. Advances in satellite imagery and Artificial Intelligence (AI) have enhanced our capabilities for early detection and mitigation of such hazards. In this paper, we propose a marine event detection pipeline for Earth observation satellites equipped with multi- or hyperspectral sensors. Our approach includes a self-supervised neural network encoder that compresses satellite images into a reduced latent space, enabling efficient onboard processing. A machine learning anomaly detection model identifies deviations from normal sea patterns to detect environmental anomalies. We compare its performance against traditional algorithms such as Isolation Forest, One-Class Support Vector Machine and Local Outlier Factors. Our lightweight, resource-efficient pipeline is optimized for deployment on satellites with limited computational resources, ranging from embedded CPUs to AI hardware accelerators. By prioritizing the transmission of critical information, our solution enhances system responsiveness and optimizes satellite communication bandwidth. Demonstrated through current integration across multiple missions, including European Space Agency's (ESA) Phisat-2 mission and Microsoft/Thales Alenia Space IMAGIN-e mission, our pipeline aims to improve marine environmental monitoring by providing timely alerts and efficient data reduction.

</details>


### [95] [ProAR: Learning Prospective Reasoning with Autoregressive Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03664)
*Linghui Shen,Tinghui Zhu,Sheng Zhang,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProAR将自回归视频生成转化为目标导向的推理过程，通过目标帧预测与未来表征自对齐，用仅25%训练步数超越完整训练的AR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型擅长因果生成，但依赖下一块预测导致其局限于短视、被动的范式。对于以推理为导向的生成任务，达成目标结果并经过有效中间状态比局部视觉合理性更重要，因此需要用目标导向推理来克服这一局限。

Method: 提出ProAR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过非对称注意力掩码将目标帧预测整合进自回归循环，使预测的目标帧引导中间状态生成且不被其干扰，实现长程目标锚定；(2) 引入未来表征自对齐，鼓励当前隐藏状态预测即将到来的时序动态，利用教师强制在单次前向传播中提取干净的未来表征，并用轻量级、仅训练期使用的预测器进行对齐。二者结合稀疏显式目标监督与稠密隐式逐步引导。

Result: 在多种视觉推理基准上，ProAR的互补组件持续提升性能；仅用25%的训练步数即超过完全训练的标准AR基线，训练效率高；在具身推理任务上也展现出良好适用性。

Conclusion: ProAR以适中的计算成本实现了连贯、目标导向的推理进展，证明将自回归视频生成重构为目标导向推理过程的有效性与训练高效性，并可扩展到具身推理等任务。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video models excel at causal generation, but their reliance on next-chunk prediction confines them to a short-sighted, reactive paradigm. This limitation is particularly consequential for reasoning-oriented generation, where achieving a target outcome through valid intermediate states matters more than local visual plausibility. To address this challenge, we propose Learning Prospective Reasoning with Autoregressive Video Models (ProAR), a novel framework that transforms autoregressive video generation into a goal-oriented reasoning process. ProAR introduces two key components: (1) To anchor generation to the long-range outcome, we integrate goal-frame prediction into the autoregressive loop via an asymmetric attention mask, enabling the predicted goal frame to guide the generation of intermediate states without being disrupted by them. (2) To guide short-range transitions, we introduce future representation self-alignment to encourage current hidden states to anticipate upcoming temporal dynamics. By leveraging teacher-forcing in AR training, we extract clean future representations in a single forward pass and align current representations with them using a lightweight, training-only predictor. Together, these two mechanisms seamlessly combine explicit, sparse target supervision with implicit, dense step-wise guidance, promoting coherent, goal-directed reasoning progress with modest computational cost. Experiments show that ProAR's complementary components consistently improve performance across diverse visual reasoning benchmarks. The framework proves highly training-efficient, surpassing fully trained standard AR baselines using only 25% of the training steps. This paradigm also demonstrates promising applicability to embodied reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [96] [SigLIP2 for aerial fire risk classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03689)
*Yunus Serhat Bıçakçı*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将预训练SigLIP2图像编码器迁移至航空影像的七类火灾风险分类任务，比较冻结编码器探针与全模型微调两种方案，并提供了可复现的数据划分与实验框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练视觉编码器（SigLIP2）在航空影像火灾风险分类上的迁移能力，并建立可复现的实验流程，以便后续与其他视觉编码器进行公平比较。

Method: 对公开FireRisk训练镜像进行可复现的数据划分；实现记录数据来源、预处理与模型选择的流程；开展两组初始实验：冻结编码器探针（probe）与全模型适配（full adaptation），均使用单一训练种子，并在同一验证集上选择检查点。

Result: 在验证集上，全模型适配达到63.05%准确率和58.94%宏F1，优于探针方案的55.95%和50.19%。

Conclusion: 这些初步结果支持对SigLIP2开展进一步评估，但尚不能证实其在独立测试集或未见区域上的性能；配套代码为重复实验及与更多视觉编码器的比较提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We examine the transfer of a pretrained SigLIP2 image encoder to seven class fire risk classification from aerial imagery. We introduce a reproducible partition of the public FireRisk training mirror and an implementation that records data provenance, preprocessing and model selection. Two initial runs compare a frozen encoder probe with full model adaptation. On the validation partition, full adaptation reaches 63.05% accuracy and 58.94% macro F1, compared with 55.95% and 50.19% for the probe. Both runs use one training seed and select their checkpoint on the same validation partition. These development results support further evaluation of SigLIP2 but do not establish performance on an independent test set or unseen regions. The accompanying code provides a common framework for repeated experiments and comparisons with additional visual encoders.

</details>


### [97] [FlowHMR: Physically Plausible Motion Capture from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03691)
*Zhanke Wang,Chengfeng Zhao,Qing Shuai,Jingzhong Lin,Heng Li,Zeyu Ling,Yuxin Wen,Jing Li,Di Kang,Chunchao Guo,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowHMR将单目视频人体运动捕捉建模为视频条件下的运动生成问题，先用流匹配预训练，再用GRPO结合保真度与可追踪性奖励后训练，并发布Wild-4K数据集，在整体保真度与物理可追踪性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频恢复全局3D人体运动存在深度歧义，直接回归易塌缩到平均解，且恢复的运动不保证物理合理，导致物理轨迹跟踪常失败。

Method: 将视频动作捕捉表述为视频条件运动生成，先预训练流匹配模型生成多样候选；再用GRPO后训练，引入保真度奖励（与输入视频一致）和追踪奖励（物理控制器可成功跟踪），以调整输出偏好。

Result: 在Wild-4K（约4K互联网视频数据集）上，定性定量实验显示方法在整体运动保真度上优于SOTA，物理跟踪成功率达82.47%，最强基线GVHMR为62.82%。

Conclusion: 通过生成式建模与强化学习后训练结合物理可追踪性奖励，可有效缓解深度歧义与物理不合理问题，提升野外单目人体运动恢复的保真度与可跟踪性。

Abstract: We present FlowHMR, a framework for recovering physically plausible global 3D human motion from monocular video. Previous learning-based methods typically regress human motion directly from video and train the network with geometric supervision. However, recovering human motion from monocular video is inherently ambiguous in depth, and direct regression tends to collapse toward an averaged solution. Moreover, the recovered motions are not guaranteed to be physically plausible, so physics-based tracking of them often fails. To address these challenges, we formulate video motion capture as a video-conditioned motion generation problem and first pretrain a flow matching model for this task. Given an input video, the pretrained model generates diverse motion candidates, but not all of them are faithful to the video or physically trackable. We therefore post-train the model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with two rewards. A fidelity reward encourages consistency with the input video. A tracking reward favors motions that a physics-based controller can track successfully. Together, these rewards shift the model's output preference, so the post-trained model stays faithful to the input video while producing more physically plausible motion. We further introduce Wild-4K, a large and diverse dataset of about 4K internet videos, for evaluating human motion recovery in the wild. Qualitative and quantitative experiments on Wild-4K show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods in overall motion fidelity and achieves a physical tracking success rate of 82.47%, compared with 62.82% for the strongest baseline, GVHMR.

</details>


### [98] [Decoding the Functional Roles of Register and High-Norm Patch Tokens in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03698)
*Neel Varma,Andrew Rufail,Dipika Khullar,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器分析DINOv2中寄存器令牌与离群令牌的激活，发现二者存在语义与功能上的分工：寄存器令牌偏向高层语义概念且功能关键，离群令牌偏向低层结构纹理且功能影响微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督ViT（如DINOv2）虽学习到丰富视觉表征，但内部令牌的功能尚不清晰。近期架构引入专用寄存器令牌以抑制背景区域出现的高范数离群patch令牌，然而这两类令牌的语义与功能角色仍未被充分阐明。

Method: 在DINOv2的寄存器令牌与离群令牌激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs），并结合自动化可解释性流程、UMAP聚类和CLIP空间交叉验证进行分析；同时通过因果消融实验检验不同令牌特征的功能贡献。

Result: 寄存器令牌特征更强地关联高层语义概念，而离群令牌特征更多关联低层结构、背景及纹理主导模式。因果消融显示显著功能不对称：破坏寄存器衍生特征使表征余弦相似度下降48.17%，而破坏离群衍生特征仅下降0.31%。

Conclusion: 研究结果为自监督ViT中的令牌特化提供了证据，表明不同类型令牌在语义编码与功能作用上存在明确分工。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs), such as DINOv2, learn rich visual representations, but the functions of their internal tokens remain poorly understood. Recent architectures introduce dedicated register tokens to reduce high-norm out- lier patch tokens that emerge in background re- gions, yet the semantic and functional roles of both token types have not been fully established. In this paper, we analyze these roles by training sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on register-token and outlier-token activations in DINOv2. Using an automated interpretability pipeline, UMAP clus- tering, and CLIP-space cross-checks, we find that register-token features are more strongly associ- ated with high-level semantic concepts. Outlier- token features, by contrast, are more often associ- ated with lower-level structural, background, and texture-dominant patterns. Causal ablations fur- ther reveal a substantial functional asymmetry: disrupting top-activating register-derived features produces a 48.17% drop in representation cosine similarity, whereas disrupting outlier-derived fea- tures produces only a 0.31% drop. Together, our results provide evidence for token specialization in self-supervised ViTs.

</details>


### [99] [4DCodeBench: Benchmarking Agents on Inverse Graphics of Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03715)
*Ruihong Shen,Žiga Kovačič,Peter Kulits,Xingrui Wang,Zizhang Li,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Alan Yuille,Jieneng Chen,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出4DCodeBench基准，通过代码生成实现4D逆图形，让智能体从视频重建动态场景为可执行图形程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估智能体将视觉观测转化为场景结构与动态的紧凑表示（如物理模拟抽象）的能力，目前此类能力缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 整理真实世界视频并构建涵盖形变、流体流动、断裂等多种物理现象的合成场景，对前沿模型进行广泛基准测试。

Result: 发现强大的静态重建能力尚无法转化为对复杂动态的可靠重建。

Conclusion: 4DCodeBench为追踪智能体通过代码理解世界动态的进展提供了测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce 4DCodeBench, a benchmark for 4D inverse graphics through code generation, in which agents reconstruct dynamic scenes from video as executable graphics programs. To accomplish this, agents must translate visual observations into compact representations of scene structure and dynamics, by implementing abstractions such as physical simulations to reproduce complex behavior. To evaluate this capability, we curate a set of real-world videos and construct synthetic scenes spanning diverse physical phenomena, including deformation, fluid flow, and fracture. We perform extensive benchmarking of frontier models, finding that strong static reconstruction capabilities do not yet translate into reliable reconstruction of complex dynamics. 4DCodeBench provides a testbed for tracking progress toward agents that can interpret the dynamics of the world through code. Our benchmark is available at https://github.com/4DCodeBench/4DCodeBench

</details>


### [100] [MoSE3: Learning World-Space SE(3) at Every Pixel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03716)
*Jiahuan Cheng,Zhiyi Li,Tian Xia,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du,Qianqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoSE3，一种从单目RGB视频前馈预测逐像素稠密SE(3)运动的首个模型，通过3D点轨迹与刚性嵌入两个中间量联合学习，并构建合成数据集Art-Kubric。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密3D点跟踪仅给出每像素3自由度平移曲线，只能描述像素去向，无法刻画部件的旋转，也无法判别哪些像素作为同一刚体共同运动。逐像素SE(3)可同时提供旋转、平移与分组信息，但直接预测困难：旋转位于弯曲流形上不适合欧氏回归，且SE(3)标注极难获取。

Method: MoSE3通过两个联合学习的中间量（3D点轨迹与刚性嵌入）预测逐像素SE(3)，在软刚性聚类内以可微分方式拟合变换，从而支持端到端预测与监督。同时构建大规模合成数据集Art-Kubric，为具有丰富物理交互的关节物体提供稠密SE(3)与刚性标签。

Result: 在刚性与关节基准上，MoSE3在像素、部件和物体三个级别的SE(3)估计均达到最优；在三个数据集上的平均3D点跟踪精度达到最优；尽管仅用合成运动数据训练，对真实视频仍展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 逐像素SE(3)运动提供了更丰富的场景运动表示，MoSE3证明了通过点轨迹与刚性嵌入等中间量间接预测SE(3)的可行性，并借助合成数据弥补标注缺口，实现优于现有方法的稠密SE(3)估计与3D点跟踪。

Abstract: Dense 3D point tracking has been a prominent paradigm for modeling motion in dynamic scenes, but a point track is just a 3-DoF translation curve per pixel: it captures where pixels go, not the rotation of the underlying part, nor which pixels move together as one body. We propose MoSE3, the first feed-forward model that predicts dense SE(3) motion from monocular RGB video, producing full 6-DoF rigid transforms at every pixel in world space. Per-pixel SE(3) motion offers a richer view of how a scene moves: rotation, translation, and grouping all at once. Directly predicting SE(3) is challenging: rotations lie on a curved manifold that is ill-suited to Euclidean regression, and annotations for SE(3) are particularly difficult to acquire. To address these challenges, MoSE3 predicts per-pixel SE(3) through two jointly learned intermediates, 3D point tracks and rigidity embeddings, and recovers SE(3) by differentiably fitting transforms within each soft rigid cluster, enabling end-to-end prediction and supervision. To close the data gap, we introduce Art-Kubric, a large-scale synthetic dataset with dense SE(3) and rigidity labels for articulated objects with rich physical interactions. MoSE3 achieves state-of-the-art SE(3) estimation at pixel, part, and object levels on both rigid and articulated benchmarks, and state-of-the-art average 3D point tracking accuracy across three datasets, while showing strong generalization to real-world videos despite being trained solely on synthetic motion data.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Awomo-SimDataEngine: Agentic Simulation-ReadyWorld Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02274)
*Awomo-PhysicalRSI Team,Danjiao Ma,Enhui Ma,Haohan Liu,Heng Jia,Hui Shan,Jianhua Xu,Jiahuan Zhang,Jiangdi Xu,Kaiwen Guo,Kaicheng Yu,Linwei Zhang,Liyang Jin,Maochun Luo,Pengyao Niu,Shiwen Li,Shuangyu Feng,Tong Zhang,Tianheng Wang,Xin Wang,Xiangru Huang,Yongqiang Huang,Zhaozhi Wang,Zijian Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Awomo-SimDataEngine，一个将资产生成、场景生成与机器人演示合成连接起来的智能体系统，用于生成可交互、物理有效、可重复执行的机器人训练数据，并验证其跨仿真器策略训练的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成有用的机器人训练数据不仅需要视觉上合理的场景，还要求物体支持交互、摆放保持物理有效、任务可被重复执行。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求，因此需要一个端到端系统将资产生成与场景生成连接到机器人演示合成。

Method: 系统包含共享资产服务，通过 ISArt 提供刚性/铰接物体及基于结构的分部件与关节生成；场景生成有两条路线：Unravel 从图像重建可编辑场景，SimForge 从文本构建单房间与多房间环境；图原生调度框架协调构建、验证与有界修复，将失败路由至对应模块并保留未受影响的场景状态；PolicyForge 将验证过的世界与任务和机器人本体绑定，生成可重放的演示数据。

Result: 评估涵盖资产几何、场景质量与下游策略学习。在基于 MuJoCo 的 LIBERO-Plus 上，与 Isaac Sim 演示联合训练使 World-Action Model (WAM) 的整体成功率从 77.17% 提升至 89.43%，目标成功率与空间成功率分别提升 31.66 和 6.25 个百分点；长时序任务上增益较为有限。

Conclusion: 结果支持所生成数据在跨仿真器策略训练中的实用性，尤其在目标与空间任务上效果显著，但长时序任务仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Generating useful robot-training data requires more than visually plausiblescenes: objects must support interaction, placements must remain physicallyvalid, and tasks must admit repeatable execution. We present\textbf{Awomo-SimDataEngine}, an agentic system that connects asset and scenegeneration to robot demonstration synthesis. Shared asset services providerigid and articulated objects, including structure-grounded part and jointgeneration with ISArt. Scene generation supports two complementary routes:Unravel reconstructs editable scenes from images, while SimForge buildssingle-room and multi-room environments from text. A graph-native harnesscoordinates construction, validation, andbounded repair, routing failures to the responsible module while retainingunaffected scene state. PolicyForge binds validated worlds to tasks and robotembodiments to produce replayable demonstrations. Evaluations cover assetgeometry, scene quality, and downstream policy learning. On MuJoCo-basedLIBERO-Plus, co-training with Isaac Sim demonstrations improves the overallsuccess rate of a World-Action Model (WAM) from $77.17\%$ to $89.43\%$. Goal and spatialsuccess improve by $31.66$ and $6.25$ percentage points, respectively.These results support the utility of the generated data for cross-simulatorpolicy training, with more limited gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [102] [World-Calibrated Proposal-to-Action Flow for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02323)
*Jie He,Wei Li,Junwen Tong,Rui Shao,Wei-Shi Zheng,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProAct提出"世界校准的proposal-to-action"框架，将生成源本身变得可预测，通过提案专家与世界专家协同校准各向异性源分布，在提升性能的同时减少50%去噪步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的VLA策略从任务无关的各向同性高斯源生成动作块，该源既不依赖近期执行历史，也不依赖预测的未来演化：一是丢弃了近期运动建立的局部连续性；二是即使引入预测性世界表示，也往往只条件化传输动力学，而无法决定生成起点、允许的偏离范围及可扩展的动作方向。

Method: ProAct包含三个部分：(i) 轻量级Proposal Expert将近期动作通过一次以运动为锚的端点流匹配步骤，转换为场景感知假设，使生成初始靠近示范动作流形；(ii) 前瞻性World Expert将该假设作为软运动先验，预测任务一致的潜在未来，联合捕捉预期场景演化与提案-未来兼容性；(iii) 基于该兼容性校准以提案为中心的各向异性源，用有界的每步幅度控制允许偏离，并在条件数预算下以迹归一化低秩几何，在耦合的平移、旋转与夹爪方向上分配细化。

Result: 与π_{0.5}相比，ProAct在仿真和真实世界任务中均提升性能，同时减少50%去噪步数、最高降低25.8%推理延迟、最高提升34.8%吞吐量。

Conclusion: 将生成源本身设计为可预测、由世界模型校准的以提案为中心的各向异性分布，可在保证运动连续性与场景一致性的同时显著提升效率，是一种兼顾性能与推理速度的VLA策略生成范式。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies generate action chunks by transporting samples from a task-agnostic isotropic Gaussian source. As this source is conditioned on neither recent execution nor predicted future evolution, (i) it discards the local continuity established by recently executed motion. (ii) Even when predictive world representations are introduced, they often only condition the transport dynamics rather than determine where generation starts, how far it may deviate, or along which action directions it may expand. Building on this observation, we introduce ProAct, a world-calibrated proposal-to-action framework that makes the generative source itself predictable. (i) To preserve motion continuity, a lightweight Proposal Expert converts recent actions into a scene-aware hypothesis via one motion-anchored endpoint flow-matching step, initializing generation near the demonstrated action manifold. (ii) To jointly capture intended scene evolution and proposal-future compatibility, a prospective World Expert treats the hypothesis as a soft motion prior while predicting the task-consistent latent future. (iii) From this compatibility, the model calibrates a proposal-centered anisotropic source, where a bounded per-step extent controls the allowed deviation and a trace-normalized low-rank geometry under a condition-number budget allocates refinement over coupled translation, rotation, and gripper directions. Compared with $π_{0.5}$, ProAct improves performance across simulation and real-world tasks while reducing denoising steps by 50%, inference latency by up to 25.8%, and increasing throughput by up to 34.8%.

</details>


### [103] [SoTa: Soft Tactile Skins for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02338)
*Jingyun Yang,Baiyu Shi,Timothy Yu,Haitian Liu,Alberta Longhini,Weichen Wang,Rika Antonova,Zhenan Bao,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SoTa，一种低成本电容式触觉皮肤，可在人手与机器人手上提供全手覆盖且共享 202 触觉单元布局，用于人-机器人协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-触觉机器人数据稀缺，因为灵巧演示依赖遥操作，限制了数据规模。人类演示采集成本低得多，但要利用它，人手和机器人手需配备对应信号的触觉传感器，这要求传感器能贴合不同手部几何、覆盖全手，并在不同本体上共享布局。

Method: 设计多层结构、织物电极的电容式触觉皮肤，可自制薄而柔软的皮肤并定制几何形状；在对应手指和手掌区域保持 202 个触觉单元的共享布局，从而使用统一触觉编码器进行人-机器人协同训练，无需学习跨传感器映射。

Result: 每片皮肤材料成本低于 10 美元；10,000 次加载-卸载循环后保留超过 97% 初始响应范围，1,280 次握拳折叠循环后轨迹仍连续。在三个接触丰富操作任务中，触觉观测提升分布内成功率；固定机器人演示预算下，加入人类演示使八种评估条件的平均成功率从 22.8% 提升至 45.9%，并在所有五个分布外条件下均取得提升。

Conclusion: SoTa 以低成本、可定制、全手覆盖且跨本体共享布局的触觉皮肤，实现了人-机器人协同训练，显著提升接触丰富操作任务的成功率。作者计划开源制造和操作这些皮肤所需资源。

Abstract: A growing body of work suggests that tactile sensing gives robot policies contact information that complements vision in dexterous manipulation. However, visuo-tactile robot data remains scarce: dexterous demonstrations require teleoperating robots, which limits dataset scale. Human demonstrations are far cheaper to collect and offer a path to scale this data, but only if human and robot hands carry tactile sensors with corresponding signals. This requires sensors that conform to different hand geometries, cover the full hand, and share a common layout across embodiments. We present SoTa, a low-cost capacitive tactile skin that provides full-hand coverage on humans and robots while preserving a shared layout of 202 taxels across corresponding finger and palm regions. Our multilayer design with fabric electrodes enables in-house fabrication of thin, soft skins with customizable geometry for under $10 in materials per skin. The sensor retains over 97% of its initial response span after 10,000 loading-unloading cycles with traces retaining continuity through 1,280 tight-fist folding cycles. The shared taxel layout supports human-robot co-training with a common tactile encoder and no learned cross-sensor mapping. Across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, tactile observations improve in-distribution success over vision-only policies. With a fixed robot demonstration budget, adding human demonstrations more than doubles mean success across eight evaluation conditions, from 22.8% to 45.9%, improving success in all five out-of-distribution conditions. We plan to open-source the resources needed to fabricate and operate these skins.

</details>


### [104] [NEEDLEWORK: Offline Rewriting of Robot Data with Verified Local Stitches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02339)
*Juntao Ren,Yifan Hou,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEEDLE是一种离线数据集增强算法，通过在高维机器人示教中记录观测之间添加经过验证的短动作桥，将低效或失败的示教轨迹拼接为更优的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示教中即使单条轨迹低效或失败也可能包含有用行为。轨迹拼接可组合这些行为改进训练数据，但高维机器人数据中识别有效连接并验证其可行性困难，已有方法多依赖低维状态表示。

Method: NEEDLE仅在记录的观测之间添加短的、经过验证的动作桥。它识别并创建能绕过次优绕路、扩大动作覆盖、并用失败轨迹增强原数据集的连接，仅使用RGB图像、本体感知和回合级结果，无需新的环境交互或特权物体状态。随后提出采样技术，将接受的桥纳入策略训练，不合成中间图像、不丢弃原始示教，使策略学习替代动作同时保留原数据覆盖。

Result: 在真实机器人任务上，NEEDLE相比每个任务上最强基线平均提升21个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: NEEDLE证明无需低维状态、额外交互或合成图像，即可通过验证的动作桥实现高维机器人示教的有效数据集增强，显著提升策略成功率。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations may contain useful behavior even when individual episodes are inefficient or unsuccessful. Trajectory stitching offers a way to compose these behaviors into improved training data, but identifying useful connections and verifying their feasibility is difficult in high-dimensional robot data, where many prior methods rely on low-dimensional state representations. We introduce NEEDLE, an offline dataset-augmentation algorithm that addresses these challenges by adding short, verified action bridges between recorded observations in high-dimensional robot demonstrations. First, NEEDLE identifies and creates connections that bypass suboptimal detours, broaden action coverage, and augment the original dataset with failed trajectories, using only RGB images, proprioception, and episode-level outcomes, without new environment interaction or privileged object state. Next, we present a sampling technique that incorporates accepted bridges into policy training without synthesizing intermediate images or discarding the original demonstrations, allowing policies to learn alternative actions while retaining the original dataset's coverage. On real-robot tasks, NEEDLE improves success rate over the strongest baseline on each task by an average of 21 percentage points. Videos and supplementary materials are on https://needle-work.github.io/.

</details>


### [105] [Filter-Aware Fine-Tuning for Safe Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02341)
*Pranit Mohnot,Christian Helten,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示追踪策略与安全过滤器独立处理会导致动力学、目标与信息层面的根本性错配，并提出过滤感知微调方法 CoFiT 来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在非结构化环境中需要安全的全身上下运动。当前控制通常将参考生成与执行分离：由规划器、遥操作或运动生成器给出参考，强化学习策略进行动态可行的全身追踪。运行时安全过滤器（如控制屏障函数 CBF）是施加新约束的有前景手段，但作者指出将追踪策略与安全过滤器独立对待会带来根本性错配——过滤不仅改变执行动作，也改变诱导出的状态分布。

Method: 通过案例研究剖析策略-过滤器接口，分别隔离动力学错配、目标错配和信息错配，揭示其根本成因；基于这些洞见提出 CoFiT（Constrained Filter-aware Tuning），一种面向预训练追踪器的过滤感知微调方法。

Result: 在多种约束场景下，CoFiT 相比仅训练过滤器的方式，将违反约束时间在 TWIST2 上减少 91%，在 SONIC 上减少 21%，同时所需安全过滤修正更小。在 Unitree G1 硬件上，CoFiT 将 TWIST2 的违反时间减少 83%，所有试验均无需操作员干预完成，而基线有 50% 的试验需要操作员急停。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于策略-过滤器交互的可操作洞见，并确立了将学习型追踪器与运行时安全过滤器集成的设计原则。

Abstract: Safe whole-body motion is essential for deploying humanoid robots in unstructured environments. Modern humanoid control commonly separates reference specification from execution, with a planner, teleoperator, or motion generator providing a reference that a reinforcement-learning policy tracks through dynamically feasible whole-body control. Runtime safety filters, such as control barrier functions (CBFs), offer a promising approach for enforcing newly introduced constraints via interventions on the tracker's outputs. We show, however, that treating the tracking policy and safety filter independently induces fundamental mismatches, as filtering alters both the executed actions and the induced state distribution. We study this policy-filter interface through case studies that isolate dynamics, objective, and information mismatches, highlight their root causes, and use these insights to develop CoFiT (Constrained Filter-aware Tuning), a filter-aware fine-tuning method for pretrained trackers. Across diverse constraint scenes, CoFiT reduces violation time relative to filter-only training by 91% on TWIST2 and 21% on SONIC, while requiring smaller safety filter corrections. On Unitree G1 hardware, CoFiT reduces violation time by 83% for TWIST2 and completes every trial without operator intervention, whereas 50% of baseline trials require an operator stop. Together, these results provide actionable insights into policy-filter interactions and establish design principles for integrating learned trackers with runtime safety filters.

</details>


### [106] [SocialVLA: A Social Perception Gateway for Human-Reaction-Based Failure Detection and Recovery in VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02360)
*Sofya Konstantinova,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SocialVLA 是一个本地、与策略无关的社会感知网关，将人类自发的社交反应转化为 VLA 机器人操作的运行时中断信号，并在物理机器人上验证了从社会反应到中断与恢复的完整本地闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略虽能完成多样化机器人操作，但执行中无法识别自身错误，容易导致失败；而人类旁观者在失败完成前就会通过声音、表情或语言产生自发反应，这一互补信号可用于及时干预。

Method: SocialVLA 融合因果副语言音频检测、视觉反应识别、显式停止短语和机器人相关性估计；采用异步首次事件融合机制，以最早且足够置信的信号触发 VLA 暂停，另设独立语音通道捕捉口头纠正，用于由参与者主导的继续、重启或指令修改。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 物理操作平台上，15 名参与者、238 个标注干预片段与 1.038 小时非干预行为数据：冻结离线回放召回率 54.6%、精确率 69.5%；未过滤音视频融合召回率达 64.3%；相关性估计将误停片段从 100 降至 57，精确率由 60.5% 升至 69.8%。对未见的第 16 名参与者前瞻部署中，冻结系统召回率 59.5%、精确率 91.7%。检测器到融合中位延迟 47.9 ms，VLA 门控到物理中止延迟 336 ms，反应发生到中止延迟 1.021 s。

Conclusion: 结果证明存在一条完整的本地通路，可将人类自发社会反应转化为物理层面的 VLA 中断，并支持由参与者主导的恢复，为 VLA 操作提供了策略无关的运行时纠错机制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies enable diverse robotic manipulation but can fail during execution without recognizing their own errors. Human observers provide complementary signals, as unexpected robot behavior can trigger rapid vocal, facial, or verbal reactions before failure is completed. We introduce SocialVLA, a local, policy-agnostic social perception gateway that converts spontaneous human reactions into runtime intervention signals for VLA manipulation. SocialVLA combines causal paralinguistic audio detection, visual reaction recognition, explicit stop phrases, and robot-relevance estimation. An asynchronous first-event fusion mechanism triggers a VLA hold from the earliest sufficiently confident signal, while a separate speech channel captures verbal corrections for participant-directed continuation, restart, or instruction revision. We evaluate SocialVLA on physical Unitree G1 manipulation using 15 participants, with 238 annotated intervention-worthy episodes and 1.038 h of non-intervention behavior. Frozen offline replay achieves 54.6% recall and 69.5% precision, while unfiltered audio-video fusion reaches 64.3% recall. Relevance estimation reduces false-stop episodes from 100 to 57 and increases precision from 60.5% to 69.8%. In prospective deployment on an unseen 16th participant, the frozen system achieves 59.5% recall and 91.7% precision. Median detector-to-fusion latency is 47.9 ms, VLA-gate-to-physical-hold latency is 336 ms, and reaction-onset-to-hold latency is 1.021 s. These results demonstrate a complete local pathway from spontaneous social reaction to physical VLA interruption and participant-directed recovery.

</details>


### [107] [Rethinking World-Action Model for Compositional and In-Context Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02368)
*Shukai Gong,Xuanran Zhai,Yintianrun Zhang,Ruopeng Cui,Ye Huang,Yiyang Fu,Dexuan Lyu,Chaojie Li,Xinyi Song,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Mingyuan Jia,Yufan Deng,Jiaxin Fang,Bo Liang,Jiaxin Li,Yuxiang Gao,Hao Liu,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViGAR 是一个分层框架，将操作任务分解为视觉子目标规划器和子目标执行器，共享预训练世界模型表示，在 RoboTwin 基准和真实机器人任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型（WAMs）虽能联合预测短时程视觉未来与动作，但缺乏显式的子任务级推理，难以应对需要多个协调子任务的长时程组合操作任务，这在真实机器人部署中日益重要。

Method: 提出 ViGAR，将操作任务分解为视觉子目标规划器和子目标执行器两部分。子目标规划器根据当前观测和全局指令预测下一子任务的视觉子目标；子目标执行器以此为条件联合生成未来视觉轨迹与动作。两个组件共享预训练的世界模型表示，从而在任务级规划和动作生成中复用共同的物理知识。此外，框架天然支持上下文学习：以全局目标图像作为上下文即可在不更新参数的情况下诱导不同的子任务分解与行为。

Result: 在 RoboTwin Clean2Random 基准上，ViGAR 在 Clean 和 Random 设置下分别达到 82.00% 和 67.02% 的成功率，平均成功率超出最强基线 12.86 个百分点。在五个组合任务和两个上下文学习任务的真实机器人实验进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ViGAR 通过显式的子任务级视觉规划和共享世界模型表示，有效提升了长时程组合操作任务的表现，并支持无需参数更新的上下文学习，在仿真基准和真实机器人实验中均得到验证。

Abstract: Long-horizon compositional manipulation has become increasingly important for real-world robot deployment, where a single task involves multiple coordinated subtasks. Existing world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict short-horizon visual futures and actions, but typically lack explicit subtask-level reasoning. We propose Visual Goal-conditioned Action Reasoning (ViGAR), a hierarchical framework that factorizes manipulation into a visual subgoal planner and a subgoal executor. Given the current observation and global instruction, the subgoal planner predicts a visual subgoal for the next subtask. The subgoal executor then jointly generates future visual trajectories and actions conditioned on the predicted subgoal. Both components share a pretrained world-model representation, enabling task-level planning and action generation to benefit from common physical knowledge. Moreover, our framework naturally supports in-context learning: using a global goal image as context can induce different subtask decompositions and behaviors without parameter updates. On the RoboTwin Clean2Random benchmark, ViGAR achieves 82.00% and 67.02% success rates under the Clean and Random settings, respectively, surpassing the strongest baseline by 12.86 percentage points in average success rate. Real-world robot experiments on five compositional and two in-context learning tasks further confirm the effectiveness of ViGAR.

</details>


### [108] [Keep the Effect, Drop the Actor: Programmable Effect-to-Execution World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02398)
*Junyi Hu,Zhewen He,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"效果程序"表征：将机器人演示拆解为物体层面的3D关键点轨迹、抓取接触点对与终态场景（去除演示者），并用71.5M参数的世界-动作模型PEWAM以四条独立流匹配时间生成效果、执行、动作与终态，从而把推理转化为"编程"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示同时包含"物体发生了什么"与"某条特定机械臂如何做到"，二者纠缠导致策略难以跨本体、跨场景迁移，也难被人类直接编辑。作者希望只以"效果"为条件，剥离演示者身份，从而获得可迁移、可编辑、可闭环重解的任务表征。

Method: 将演示编译为效果程序：移动物体的3D关键点轨迹、标记每个物体被持握位置的两点、以及去除演示者后的终态场景配置。提出PEWAM（71.5M参数的世界-动作模型），把效果、机器人执行、动作、终态作为四条流，各自拥有独立的流匹配时间；固定程序并采样执行流，即可将推理变为编程，并从实时场景闭环重新求解。

Result: 在留出的LIBERO-Goal任务上，单条演示的程序完成90个episode中的40个，而相同骨干以目标图像或语言为条件、以及已发表的演示条件方法最多完成19个；在Meta-World三个留出类别上超过最佳已发表结果，但五类平均未超过。程序可人工编辑：位移跟随改变的终态，抓取随接触点旋转而转动。同一程序无需重训练即可在四种机械臂上运行；受扰动后重新求解完成60个episode中的23个，而重放演示仅完成6个。在真实Franka臂上（用其他任务的真机演示微调），由单段人类视频编译的程序完成40次试验中的36次，相同骨干以视频末帧作为目标图像仅完成22次。

Conclusion: 以"效果程序"为条件、用多流独立时间的世界-动作模型生成执行，能显著超越目标图像、语言及已有演示条件方法，并支持人类编辑、跨本体零重训练迁移与扰动后的闭环重解，还可用单段人类视频在真实机械臂上取得高成功率。

Abstract: A robot demonstration records two things in the same frames: what happened to the objects, and how one particular arm made it happen. We condition on the first. A demonstration is compiled into an effect program: the 3D keypoint trajectories of the objects that moved, two points marking where each was held, and the configuration the scene ends in, with the demonstrator removed. PEWAM, a 71.5M-parameter world-action model, generates effect, robot execution, action and terminal state as four streams with independent flow-matching times, so clamping a program and sampling the execution turns inference into programming, re-solved closed loop from the live scene. On held-out LIBERO-Goal tasks, one demonstration's program completes 40 of 90 episodes, where the same backbone given a goal image or language, and published demonstration-conditioned methods, complete at most 19; on three of Meta-World's held-out classes it exceeds the best published results, though not on the five-class mean. Because a program is a set of coordinates, a person can edit it: the placement follows a shifted terminal state and the grasp turns with rotated contact points. The same program runs on four robot arms without retraining, and after a push, re-solving completes 23 of 60 episodes where replaying the demonstration completes 6. On a Franka arm, fine-tuned on real demonstrations of other tasks, programs compiled from single human videos complete 36 of 40 trials, against 22 for the same backbone conditioned on the video's last frame as a goal image.

</details>


### [109] [Autonomous mobile robot operations logistics: a dataset of jobs, dispatch events and robot states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02428)
*Jan-Felix Klein,Yongkuk Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MoRoOp数据集，记录了自主移动机器人在实验室物料配送场景中连续九个八小时班次的操作数据，涵盖任务生成、调度与机器人控制全流程，并提供原始与清洗后的机器人状态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在生产物流中承担物料运输，其运行涉及任务生成、调度与机器人控制，但缺乏真实操作环境下可用于AI决策、扰动检测等研究的公开数据。

Method: 在实验室的套件准备与供应场景中，连续九个八小时班次记录AMR运行；机器人在每班次执行随机生成的套件供应、空箱补充和充电任务。数据关联任务规格、构成每个任务的操作、记录操作状态转换与结果的调度事件，以及包含位置、朝向、速度、各轮电量与诊断信息的机器人状态观测，并保留延迟、导航受阻和失败操作等技术上有效的观测。提供原始与清洗后的机器人状态表。

Result: 数据集包含1,382个任务、4,815个操作、19,352条调度事件和140,386条机器人状态观测，以及任务生成所用的套件规格。

Conclusion: 该数据集可用于AI智能体在运营决策记录上的评估、扰动检测、操作预测、数据驱动仿真及事件日志分析等方向。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots (AMRs) increasingly perform material transport in production logistics, where their operation is governed by job generation, dispatching and robot control. We present MoRoOp, a dataset of AMR operations recorded in a laboratory kit preparation and supply scenario over nine eight-hour shifts. During each shift, an AMR executed stochastically generated kit supply, empty-box refill and charging jobs. The dataset links job specifications, the operations constituting each job, dispatch events documenting operation state transitions and outcomes, and robot-state observations comprising position, orientation, velocity, per-wheel state of charge and diagnostics. It contains 1,382 jobs, 4,815 operations, 19,352 dispatch events and 140,386 robot-state observations together with the kit specifications used during job generation. The dataset was recorded in an operating laboratory environment, and technically valid observations of delays, obstructed navigation and unsuccessful operations were retained. Both raw and cleaned robot-state tables are provided. Documented reuse directions include the evaluation of AI agents on operational decision records, disturbance detection, operation prediction, data-driven simulation and event-log analysis.

</details>


### [110] [OpenRUA: Robot-Use Agents Are Zero-Shot Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02459)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Earl T. Barr,Claire Le Goues,Peter O'Hearn,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OpenRUA——一种"零抽象"的机器人编程框架，让现成编码智能体仅通过终端访问机器人原生接口 ROS 2，无需定制抽象层或工作流，即可作为零样本视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作为了用编码智能体控制机器人，往往构建复杂的定制化"套具"（harness），规定专门的工作流并提供专用接口。作者质疑：这种额外的套具工程是否真的必要？能否让智能体直接使用工程师数十年积累的成熟软件栈？

Method: 设计 OpenRUA，采用极简的"工作区即套具"思路：只给现成编码智能体提供终端访问 ROS 2 的能力、ROS 2 文档和基础工具，不编排任何智能体工作流，由智能体自行组织工作。在该工作区内，将感知重构为文件 I/O、将操作重构为写代码。使用 Claude Code（驱动模型为 Claude Opus 5）进行实验。

Result: 在 CaP-Bench 上成功率达 99.0%，在 LIBERO-PRO 上达 87.0%。分析发现显著涌现行为：感知方面，96.80% 的回合中智能体自发编写程序处理原始传感输入并推导度量测量；操作方面，95.87% 的回合中自发构建运动控制客户端（如夹爪控制），50.13% 的回合中构建闭环控制程序（如依据传感反馈调整运动）。

Conclusion: 现成编码智能体可仅通过机器人原生接口充当零样本视觉运动策略，无需专用原语或任务特定训练，说明复杂的定制套具工程可能并非必要。

Abstract: Coding agents are extending their reach into the physical world by writing and executing robot control programs. One might expect the agents to use the existing mature software stack that engineers have developed over decades to access sensors and control motion. Yet prior work primarily engineers complex custom harnesses to orchestrate agents for robot use, particularly by prescribing specialized workflows and providing bespoke interfaces. This raises the question: "Is such additional harness engineering necessary?" We introduce OpenRUA, a zero-abstraction harness that bypasses bespoke abstraction layers by providing off-the-shelf coding agents with only terminal access to the robot's native software interface ROS 2. OpenRUA employs a minimalist workspace-as-harness design, only offering ROS 2 documentation and basic tools while leaving the coding agent to organize its own work without orchestrating any agentic workflow. Within this workspace, OpenRUA recasts perception as file I/O and manipulation as coding. With Claude Code powered by Claude Opus 5, OpenRUA achieves success rates of 99.0% on CaP-Bench and 87.0% on LIBERO-PRO, demonstrating that an off-the-shelf coding agent can serve as a zero-shot visuomotor policy through the robot's native interface, without bespoke primitives or task-specific training. Under this minimalist design, further analysis reveals striking emergent behaviors of coding agents: (1) For perception, the agent spontaneously writes programs that process raw sensory inputs and derive metric measurements in 96.80% of episodes. (2) For manipulation, the agent spontaneously builds motion-control clients (e.g., gripper control) in 95.87% of episodes and closed-loop control programs (e.g., adjusting motion based on sensor feedback) in 50.13% of episodes. Our code is available at https://github.com/terminalworld/OpenRUA.

</details>


### [111] [RUL-Aware RRT*: Degradation-Balanced Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02469)
*Haibo Li,Zhiguo Zeng,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RUL-aware RRT*运动规划方法，将关节剩余使用寿命信息纳入规划过程，动态调整关节使用以缓解长期运行中的关节退化不均衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂长期运行会出现关节退化不均衡，最弱执行器提前失效，导致非计划停机和重大运营损失；传统运动规划不考虑关节健康状态，反而会加剧这种不均衡。

Method: 在RRT*规划框架中引入关节剩余使用寿命（RUL）信息，根据关节健康状态的演化自适应调整各关节的使用程度，并在三种场景下评估：完全健康场景（FHS）、异质退化场景（HDS）和局部退化场景（LDS）。

Result: 结果表明，RUL-aware RRT*能有效抑制退化不均衡、延迟瓶颈失效的出现，并提升机械臂系统长期运行的可靠性。

Conclusion: 将健康反馈融入运动规划，是提升持续磨损条件下机械臂耐久性与运行韧性的实用且稳健的途径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators operating over long durations often experience uneven joint degradation, which causes the weakest actuator to fail prematurely, leads to unplanned downtime, and results in significant operational losses. Traditional motion-planning algorithms do not account for joint health conditions and therefore tend to exacerbate this imbalance during extended operation. To address this challenge, this study introduces the RUL-aware RRT*, a motion-planning method that incorporates joint remaining useful life information into the planning process and adaptively adjusts joint usage in response to evolving health conditions. The method is evaluated across three representative scenarios, namely the Full Health Scenario (FHS), the Heterogeneous Degradation Scenario (HDS), and the Local Degradation Scenario (LDS). The results show that the RUL-aware RRT* effectively suppresses degradation imbalance, delays the emergence of bottleneck failures, and improves the long-term reliability of the robotic system during extended operation. These findings demonstrate that integrating health feedback into motion planning provides a practical and robust pathway for enhancing the durability and operational resilience of manipulators subject to continuous wear.

</details>


### [112] [CriticHack: Evaluating Visual Rewards Under Robot Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02527)
*Jiaxuan Luo,Xingguo Xu,Shanshan Wang,Yuhan Zhou,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 学习型视觉奖励模型可能在提升任务成功率的同时，放大了"作用对象错误"的失败模式，且常规监控指标无法察觉；这可用KL正则化下的"倾斜模型"解释，并可用冻结的结果验证器纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型视觉奖励模型被越来越多用于优化机器人策略，但奖励模型可能给"作用在错误对象上的执行"与"真正完成任务"打一样高的分。作者想揭示：优化这种奖励会放大错误对象失败，而奖励与任务成功率却同时上升，使从业者通常监控的信号看起来一切正常，从而掩盖问题。

Method: 在抽屉任务上，对一个扩散策略的每个去噪器参数针对Robometer进行微调；初始策略为无奖励暴露的监督策略。进行五组训练，与基于模拟器任务完成信号训练的五组对照。在512个评估种子上比较任务成功率与错误对象失败率，并在策略原生扩散采样器下、距初始策略等距、以及两种critic与两种optimizer的约束策略实验中复现。提出"倾斜模型"（tilt model）解释该现象，并在26个约束设置中验证其预测。

Result: 五组训练使任务成功率提升10.2个百分点，同时错误对象失败率提升10.9个百分点；而基于模拟器任务完成信号的五组训练在提升成功率的同时并未放大错误对象失败（差异9.2个百分点，95% CI 5.6–13.0）。该放大现象在多种条件下复现。Robometer整体区分成功与失败良好（AUROC 0.81），但对错误对象失败的打分略高于成功（AUROC 0.37），因此优化会同时抬高两者。倾斜模型在26个约束设置中预测结果变化（Spearman 0.89），包括任务成功率下降的情形，且Robometer自身已发表的成功终止配方也继承了该错误。冻结的结果验证器可将同一优化过程重定向到所请求的任务。

Conclusion: 优化学习型视觉奖励可能在不被常规指标察觉的情况下放大特定失败模式（如作用对象错误），这可由KL正则化下的倾斜模型解释并可跨设置预测；使用冻结的结果验证器能使优化重新对准真正的任务目标。

Abstract: Learned visual reward models are increasingly used to optimize robot policies, yet a reward model can score an execution that acts on the wrong object as highly as one that completes the task. We show that optimizing such a reward can amplify these wrong-object failures while reward and task success both rise, so the signals a practitioner would normally monitor look healthy. We fine-tune every denoiser parameter of a diffusion policy against Robometer on a drawer task.
